**[00:23:02.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000111)  
To the honorable members of the Senate of the Philippines, the distinguished members, and the Senate Committee on Finance led by the Honorable

**[00:23:13.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000113)  
Good afternoon. The Department of the Interior and Local Government respectfully appears before the

**[00:23:18.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000114)  
Honorable Committee to present our ?332.5 billion budget proposal for fiscal year 2027. Alongside

**[00:23:26.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000115)  
priorities for the coming year we will highlight our major accomplishments and financial performance

**[00:23:31.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000116)  
covering fiscal year 2025 to the first half of fiscal year 2026. We will also provide a

**[00:23:37.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000117)  
detailed breakdown of the proposed allocation by expense class outlining the resources essential

**[00:23:42.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000118)  
to implement priority programs and projects of the DILG and its attached agencies. Our achievements

**[00:23:48.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000119)  
over the past two years reflect our steadfast commitment to building safer streets fostering

**[00:23:53.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000120)  
progressive communities and empowering good local governance nationwide every single peso entrusted

**[00:23:58.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000121)  
to us will be matched with decisive action and tangible public service that will reach every

**[00:24:03.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000122)  
filipino working hand in hand across all branches of the government from the national level to the

**[00:24:08.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000123)  
heart of every barangay across the country with permission of the chair will we now present the

**[00:24:12.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000124)  
audiovisual presentation of the DILG's fiscal year 2027 budget proposal.

**[00:24:22.290] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000125)  
The Department of the Interior and Local Government is at the forefront of

**[00:24:25.970] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000126)  
advancing peace and order, public safety,

**[00:24:30.850] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000127)  
and responsive local governance

**[00:24:34.690] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000128)  
across the country.

**[00:24:42.450] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000129)  
together with its attached agencies the department works to strengthen local government units

**[00:24:48.450] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000130)  
enhance public safety and emergency response and promote inclusive programs that uplift the lives

**[00:24:55.890] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000131)  
entrusted to us by Congress, the Department has continued to fulfill its mandate and deliver

**[00:25:02.850] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000132)  
programs aligned with the President's priorities for the country. Safeguarding our collective peace

**[00:25:10.210] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000133)  
and prosperity and ensuring our people safe and secure remained among our foremost priorities.

**[00:25:18.370] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000134)  
We continue to strengthen the unified 9-1-1 system through acquiring additional assets

**[00:25:25.490] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000135)  
and expanding access to emergency services, which led to more calls, fewer prank calls,

**[00:25:33.330] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000136)  
and an improved emergency response time. The aggressive anti-criminality campaign by the PNP

**[00:25:44.350] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000137)  
led to reduced crimes throughout the country, while the NAPLCOM fulfilled its commitment of

**[00:25:50.430] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000138)  
zero backlog in cases involving police officers that helped restore accountability, integrity,

**[00:25:58.030] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000139)  
and balance.

**[00:25:58.590] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000140)  
Our unrelenting drive against illegal drugs led to billions worth of drugs seized,

**[00:26:07.310] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000141)  
thousands arrested, and more barangays nationwide cleared of drug-related threats.

**[00:26:14.910] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000142)  
This we did even as we completed development projects in conflict-affected areas

**[00:26:20.990] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000143)  
and gave former rebels more opportunities to begin anew.

**[00:26:27.070] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000144)  
Through the BJMP,

**[00:26:29.230] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000145)  
we significantly reduced jail congestion as well as provided holistic rehabilitation

**[00:26:35.470] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000146)  
and reintegration services for persons deprived of liberty.

**[00:26:41.390] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000147)  
We instituted sweeping reforms within the BFP to strengthen transparency, integrity,

**[00:26:48.590] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000148)  
and accountability, which contributed to more effective and efficient fire prevention

**[00:26:54.270] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000149)  
and suppression services, thereby reducing fire incidents,

**[00:26:58.990] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000150)  
and saving

**[00:26:59.710] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000151)  
more lives.

**[00:27:02.350] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000152)  
The Department continues to empower and transform our local government units

**[00:27:07.390] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000153)  
as capable and agile partners for national development.

**[00:27:12.270] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000154)  
Our rigorous monitoring of national and local government-funded projects

**[00:27:16.750] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000155)  
ensured the completion of thousands of projects such as farm-to-market roads, evacuation centers,

**[00:27:25.630] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000156)  
rice subsidies, water and

**[00:27:26.670] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000157)  
sanitation

**[00:27:27.390] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000158)  
systems,

**[00:27:28.590] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000159)  
and other community facilities that benefited millions of Filipino families,

**[00:27:35.050] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000160)  
especially in underserved and disadvantaged areas.

**[00:27:39.690] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000161)  
We nurtured our newly elected local officials,

**[00:27:43.510] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000162)  
as well as our barangay and sangguniang kabataan officials

**[00:27:46.790] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000163)  
through various capacity-building interventions.

**[00:27:50.910] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000164)  
We accelerated the digitalization and automation of LGU services

**[00:27:55.710] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000165)  
that led to faster and more efficient governance,

**[00:27:59.810] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000166)  
which is essential in attracting businesses and investments

**[00:28:03.350] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000167)  
and boosting economic development among LGUs.

**[00:28:07.990] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000168)  
The department likewise played a pivotal role in making our LGUs adequately prepared,

**[00:28:15.550] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000169)  
which cushioned the impact of typhoons and other calamities

**[00:28:19.330] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000170)  
that hit the various parts of the country.

**[00:28:22.330] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000171)  
These include the constant monitoring of evacuations,

**[00:28:25.710] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000172)  
evacuation centers in LGUs,

**[00:28:28.010] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000173)  
as well as the timely and strategic deployment of first responders in affected areas

**[00:28:33.510] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000174)  
to provide immediate assistance during and after disasters.

**[00:28:38.790] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000175)  
We also monitored the completion of rehabilitated and constructed facilities

**[00:28:45.140] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000176)  
and maintained the cleanliness of barangays and communities

**[00:28:48.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000177)  
to help prevent further risks to public health.

**[00:28:56.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000178)  
Uplifting the lives and welfare of every Filipino

**[00:28:59.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000179)  
has been the driving force behind our sectoral interventions.

**[00:29:04.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000180)  
We advanced the institutionalization of early childhood care and development

**[00:29:09.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000181)  
while investing in the capacity and potential of the next generation of Filipinos.

**[00:29:16.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000182)  
We also continued to address the needs of our Muslim brethren

**[00:29:20.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000183)  
while strengthening our efforts in advancing gender equality in the country

**[00:29:25.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000184)  
that are at par with or even better than those of others.

**[00:29:29.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000185)  
In addition, we have been working with other countries in Asia and across the globe.

**[00:29:34.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000186)  
These and much more have been made possible

**[00:29:37.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000187)  
as reflected in our strong financial performance in 2025

**[00:29:42.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000188)  
with a 96% obligation rate and a 95% disbursement rate.

**[00:29:51.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000189)  
Similarly, we remain on track to achieve our financial targets for the year

**[00:29:57.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000190)  
with a 55% obligation rate and a

**[00:29:59.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000191)  
nearly 97% disbursement rate as of mid-year.

**[00:30:07.370] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000192)  
Our proposal for the next fiscal year reflects our commitment to sustaining our ongoing initiatives

**[00:30:14.390] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000193)  
and advancing our goals, amounting to a total of P332.5 billion, a 7% increase from last

**[00:30:24.770] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000194)  
year's appropriation.

**[00:30:28.130] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000195)  
Of this amount, Bureau of Jail Management and Penology, Bureau of Fire Protection, Philippine

**[00:30:35.170] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000196)  
National Police, and the Office of the Secretary have registered increases in their respective

**[00:30:41.770] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000197)  
appropriations.

**[00:30:43.470] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000198)  
While the Early Childhood Care and Development Council, Philippine Commission on Women, National

**[00:30:49.770] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000199)  
Commission on Muslim Filipinos, National Youth Commission, and National Police Commission

**[00:30:56.210] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000200)  
have experienced decreases.

**[00:30:58.110] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000201)  
Compared with their previous appropriations.

**[00:31:02.590] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000202)  
Consistent with the previous years, the majority of our proposed budget, equivalent to 84%,

**[00:31:09.710] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000203)  
is allocated to personal services, while 13% is earmarked for maintenance and other operating

**[00:31:17.250] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000204)  
expenses and 3% for capital outlay.

**[00:31:23.270] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000205)  
The proposed budget is broken down by

**[00:31:25.150] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000206)  
expense class per agency.

**[00:31:33.960] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000213)  
With your help, we will be able to build on these gains through the proposals each agency

**[00:31:37.600] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000214)  
has put forward for your consideration.

**[00:31:42.590] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000215)  
The Office of the Secretary will continue to uplift local governance in every community

**[00:31:49.070] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000216)  
through the implementation of various programs and projects that strengthen LGU capacity,

**[00:31:55.750] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000217)  
hasten implementation of development programs, advance digitalization, and improve the quality

**[00:31:59.850] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000218)  
good governance,

**[00:32:01.370] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000219)  
and citizen

**[00:32:02.370] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000220)  
participation.

**[00:32:02.870] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000221)  
We will remain unwavering

**[00:32:04.510] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000222)  
in improving our emergency

**[00:32:05.430] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000223)  
response system to make it

**[00:32:07.130] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000224)  
more efficient and accessible

**[00:32:09.910] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000225)  
while strengthening the fight against illegal drugs and advancing peace and order in the communities.

**[00:32:24.210] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000226)  
Our drive continues to focus on the rehabilitation and cleanup of Manila

**[00:32:29.210] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000227)  
Bay, strengthening

**[00:32:30.210] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000228)  
disaster response

**[00:32:31.210] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000229)  
and preparedness, enhancing the department's ICT capabilities, and fully operationalizing

**[00:32:38.510] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000230)  
our new central office and regional offices. The LGA will continue to strengthen LGUs and

**[00:32:49.640] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000231)  
the DILG personnel through training newly elected barangay officials, advancing disaster readiness,

**[00:32:56.980] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000232)  
innovation, creative industries, and security, and strengthening their capacity to deliver these

**[00:33:05.260] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000233)  
interventions. To uphold integrity and trust in our police force, the NAPOLCOM will prioritize

**[00:33:14.800] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000234)  
investments in critical facilities in Regions 3, 5, and Cordillera Administrative Region,

**[00:33:22.520] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000235)  
ICT projects, ISO certification, and equipment for Negros Island Region.

**[00:33:29.060] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000236)  
The Philippine National Police will strengthen its ranks to become a reliable and trustworthy

**[00:33:38.010] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000237)  
partner in law enforcement through the recruitment of new personnel, enhanced equipment and ICT

**[00:33:45.810] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000238)  
capabilities, and stronger support for the welfare of its personnel. This will also include

**[00:33:55.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000239)  
fulfilling commitments under the Program Convergence Initiatives, intensifying police

**[00:34:00.740] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000240)  
operations, and constructing new facilities.

**[00:34:03.920] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000241)  
The Bureau of Fire Protection will likewise accelerate efforts in fire prevention,

**[00:34:12.360] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000242)  
suppression, and emergency response through hiring new fire officers, operationalizing newly

**[00:34:19.740] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000243)  
completed fire stations, deploying new fire trucks, procuring equipment, and constructing

**[00:34:26.440] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000244)  
new fire stations, training centers, and a fire arson laboratory. To safeguard and rehabilitate

**[00:34:36.980] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000245)  
To safeguard and rehabilitate

**[00:34:39.200] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000248)  
persons deprived of liberty, the Bureau of Jail Management and Penology will bring in

**[00:34:43.700] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000249)  
recruits to reinforce operational capability and ensure hygiene and medical support.

**[00:34:51.260] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000250)  
It will also counter illegal drugs in our jails, procure new firearms, radios, and vans,

**[00:34:59.020] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000251)  
and construct new jail facilities in Calabarzon, molding the minds of our future public order

**[00:35:08.580] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000252)  
and safety leaders.

**[00:35:10.320] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000253)  
order and safety leaders, the Philippine

**[00:35:11.300] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000254)  
Public Safety College will continue its education and training programs,

**[00:35:16.020] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000255)  
research and curriculum development, while undertaking the construction of facilities

**[00:35:21.700] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000256)  
and procurement of PPEs and ICT equipment. The National Youth Commission will lead the

**[00:35:30.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000257)  
charge in empowering the Filipino leaders of tomorrow through mandatory and continuing

**[00:35:36.480] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000258)  
training for Sangguniang Kabataan, participation in youth-oriented programs and activities,

**[00:35:43.300] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000259)  
and support for anti-bullying and youth health initiatives.

**[00:35:49.260] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000260)  
The National Commission on Muslim Filipinos includes moonsighting activities for Idil

**[00:35:55.660] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000261)  
Adha and Idil Fitir, the administration and supervision of Hajj operations, strengthening

**[00:36:02.340] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000262)  
Madaris and Islamic studies, supporting Muslim MSMEs, providing legal and paralegal assistance,

**[00:36:10.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000263)  
and promoting peace-building and dialogue

**[00:36:12.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000264)  
in

**[00:36:13.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000265)  
Muslim

**[00:36:14.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000266)  
Filipino

**[00:36:15.800] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000267)  
communities.

**[00:36:18.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000269)  
Championing the causes of women and girls, the Philippine Commission on Women will expand

**[00:36:23.920] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000270)  
its reach in the Visayas while sustaining its initiatives on gender and development.

**[00:36:29.780] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000271)  
Lastly, the Early Childhood Care and Development Council will intensify its efforts to give

**[00:36:37.980] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000272)  
every child a stronger start by capacitating intermediaries

**[00:36:41.340] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000273)  
intermediaries and

**[00:36:42.340] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000274)  
partners,

**[00:36:43.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000275)  
ensuring accreditation of ECCD service providers and monitoring child development centers.

**[00:36:51.100] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000276)  
We will continue to stand firm and unwavering in serving the interests of the Filipino people

**[00:37:00.950] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000277)  
and in carrying forward our commitment to building a nation that embodies the ideals of a Bagong Pilipinas.

**[00:37:09.950] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000278)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:39:41.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000308)  
Regarding the last issue, we are now initiating talks with the developers of Roblox.

**[00:39:47.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000309)  
The game, the games that the young children in conflict of the land play.

**[00:39:52.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000310)  
in conflict of the law, have been influenced by.

**[00:39:56.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000311)  
We are moving and requesting the NTC

**[00:39:59.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000312)  
to formally remove the access of the children from these games

**[00:40:04.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000313)

**[00:40:08.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000314)

**[00:40:12.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000315)

**[00:40:14.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000316)

**[00:40:19.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000317)  
both CICLs were influenced heavily

**[00:40:22.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000318)  
by the games that they played.

**[00:40:24.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000319)  
That is a common denominator between the two of them.

**[00:40:27.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000320)  
In other aspects, we continue to have a faster response time

**[00:40:32.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000321)  
through the unified 911.

**[00:40:36.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000322)  
The unified 911, in one year of operation,

**[00:40:39.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000323)  
received over 2 million calls.

**[00:40:41.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000324)  
That's around 60,000 calls a day.

**[00:40:43.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000325)  
And our response time is between three to seven minutes.

**[00:40:49.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000326)  
Noteworthy also is the drive of the PNP

**[00:40:53.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000327)  
in making the city safer.

**[00:40:55.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000328)  
There has been a significant drop in crimes in double digits,

**[00:40:59.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000329)  
especially in the field of physical injury.

**[00:41:04.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000330)  
And rape has gone down dramatically

**[00:41:06.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000331)  
over this past year.

**[00:41:08.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000332)  
So we continue with our mandate.

**[00:41:13.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000333)  
And if there are any other questions,

**[00:41:16.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000334)  
we're open to answer.

of the Committee on Finance.

**[01:02:53.430] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000525)

**[01:03:04.350] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000527)

**[01:03:09.270] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000528)

**[01:03:12.590] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000529)

**[01:03:16.190] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000530)

**[01:03:25.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000533)

**[01:03:28.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000534)

**[01:03:30.850] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000535)

**[01:03:38.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000537)

**[01:03:41.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000538)

**[01:03:45.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000539)

**[01:03:46.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000540)

**[01:03:48.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000541)

**[01:04:10.350] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000550)

**[01:04:12.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000551)  
Sir, I will answer that question.

**[01:04:14.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000552)  
Sir, when I entered as secretary,

**[01:04:16.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000553)  
one of the most glaring and insulting

**[01:04:21.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000554)  
episodes of my tenure

**[01:04:24.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000555)  
was that the former chief of the BFP

**[01:04:27.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000556)  
offered me a percentage

**[01:04:29.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000557)  
of every purchase of fire trucks.

**[01:04:32.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000558)  
We subsequently investigated,

**[01:04:35.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000559)  
reported it to Ombudsman,

**[01:04:37.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000560)  
and he has been dismissed from service.

**[01:04:39.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000561)  
So we did some soul-searching

**[01:04:43.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000562)  
and some reevaluation on the bidding process,

**[01:04:47.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000563)  
and we eventually...

**[01:04:51.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000564)  
What is the term? We fired?

**[01:04:53.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000565)  
No, no.

**[01:04:54.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000566)  
We removed 35 of the top officials

**[01:04:58.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000567)  
involved in the bids and awards committee of the BFP

**[01:05:02.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000568)  
because we discovered that there were

**[01:05:04.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000569)  
hundreds and hundreds of millions of anomalous deals

**[01:05:07.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000570)  
that were done prior to my incumbency.

**[01:05:10.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000571)  
So we had to do a rethinking

**[01:05:12.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000572)  
and reimagination of the process.

**[01:05:14.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000573)  
So this year, I think we'll be more effective

**[01:05:16.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000574)  
in the disbursements of the funds allocated to the BFP.

**[01:36:56.940] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001405)

**[01:36:58.420] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001406)

**[01:37:22.420] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001413)

And we made it clear that,

pagkakataon,

pagkakataon,

pagka hindi natapos yung project,

and we readily coordinate with the DPWH.

**[01:37:41.420] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001422)

Chief PNP,

kasi ang TICAS, dalawa yan eh, AFP and PNP.

**[01:39:38.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001445)  
May isang policy question po ako, Chief PNP and to our DILG Secretary.

**[01:39:44.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001446)  
Over the past years, pag titignan ho natin yung mga surveys na lumalabas na Pulse Asia, SWS,

**[01:39:53.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001447)  
ang criminality drugs, below top 5 na siya.

**[01:39:57.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001448)  
But slowly, umakit ang umakit. I'm sure you've seen the surveys.

**[01:40:03.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001449)  
And on the ground, whenever I go around rin sa Valenzuela, naririnig ko rin na lumalakas na yung loob na naman ng mga drug pusher,

**[01:40:13.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001450)  
lumalakas yung loob ngayon ng mga petty criminals.

**[01:40:15.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001451)  
So, ano po yung mga programs natin on the ground to curb?

**[01:40:27.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001452)  
So, ano po yung mga programs natin on the ground to curb?

**[01:40:33.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001453)  
I know, tinitignan ko, yung index crime, in fairness, bumababa, but we have to sustain it.

**[01:40:39.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001454)  
So, what are the programs that the department and the agency is employing in order to sustain itong pagbaba ng mga index crimes?

**[01:40:51.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001455)  
Sir, the biggest tool that we can use is really the repealment of the Robin Padilla law.

**[01:40:57.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001456)  
We have a list of the recalcitrant violators per barangay. We know who they are.

**[01:41:04.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001457)  
But it is a state policy, and I agree with the state policy, that EJKs are no longer allowed.

**[01:41:12.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001458)  
But knowing who these recalcitrant people are, and knowing that they're in possession of illegal firearms,

**[01:41:18.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001459)  
it would greatly help us in curbing criminality if we can arrest them and hold them without bail

**[01:41:25.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001460)  
because of the illegal firearms.

**[01:41:27.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001461)  
We estimate that these are 2 million at the high level, 1 million at the low level of illegal loose firearms on the roads, in the communities.

**[01:41:38.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001462)  
We estimate that there are 42,000 barangays. If we arrest 2 per barangay, that's 84,000.

**[01:41:45.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001463)  
But we estimate we will, if given that chance, we will arrest maybe up to 150,000 more criminals and take them off the streets.

**[01:41:53.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001464)  
Sabi niya itong loose firearms.

**[01:41:57.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001465)  
Ito'y nagpapalakas ng loob ng mga criminals.

**[01:42:00.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001466)  
Yes, sir. Kasi madala, sir, pag-bail, one week later, they'll commit another crime using another firearm. Marami silang taguan.

**[01:42:08.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001467)  
And how can we amend the law or repeal, if that matter, kung kailangan, in order to stop this, itong paikot-ikot na lang na criminality?

**[01:42:21.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001468)  
Yes, sir. We have to repeal that law, sir, that was passed in 1995, if I believe.

**[01:42:27.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001469)  
I think it was 1995 when illegal firearms became bailable. So we are asking that that law be repealed and make it non-bailable again.

**[01:42:37.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001470)  
So hindi na sila natatakot? Ibig sabihin mayroon siyang loose firearms? Alam naman nila makakabail sila.

**[01:42:42.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001471)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:42:43.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001472)  
Pag-bail nila, balik ulit sa ano?

**[01:42:45.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001473)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:42:45.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001474)  
Saan nila binibili itong mga loose firearms?

**[01:42:48.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001475)  
San-san na lang, sir. May meron ang galing yung home industries na ginagawa dito sa Pilipinas.

**[01:42:55.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001476)  
Mayroon din yung mga lagi-import illegally through some portal zones of our coastlines.

**[01:43:03.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001477)  
But safe to say, sir, that if there are 2 million firearms on the loose right now, and 98% of the crimes are committed with these,

**[01:43:12.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001478)  
then we will grant an amnesty and determine whether these firearms were used in the commission of crimes.

**[01:43:19.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001479)  
But otherwise, sir, this repealment will greatly aid our nation.

**[01:43:25.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001480)  
In terms of itong mga petty drugs, ako naririnig rin, naririnig ko rin sa mga punong barangay namin sa Valenzuela,

**[01:43:37.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001481)  
na itong madaming petty drug dealings on the ground.

**[01:43:44.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001482)  
Ano bang mga plano ng agency to curb these petty drug dealings?

**[01:43:49.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001483)  
Actually, sir, if you look at the records,

**[01:43:51.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001484)  
the total consumption of the Philippines for...

**[01:43:55.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001485)  
methamphetamine, hydrochloride, is 16 tons pure.

**[01:43:59.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001486)  
It is then reduced to a...

**[01:44:01.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001487)  
pilfered down to a lower number of maybe 15% purity at the end.

**[01:44:06.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001488)  
In the last year, we have interdicted almost 11 tons, I think, of...

**[01:44:12.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001489)  
or in 2 years, we've interdicted 11 tons of shabu, pure shabu, from the market.

**[01:44:18.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001490)  
That drove up the price of shabu, but now what they're doing is lowering the purity again to maybe 5 to 8%.

**[01:44:25.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001491)  
We find that the biggest consumers are in the transport sector, which we have to address also.

**[01:44:37.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001492)

**[01:44:46.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001493)

**[01:44:53.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001494)

**[01:44:55.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001495)

**[01:45:00.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001496)

**[01:45:03.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001497)  
No, this is a very complex problem.

**[01:45:05.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001498)

**[01:45:27.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001501)

**[01:45:33.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001503)

**[01:45:34.860] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001504)  
And cascading to another crime

**[01:45:42.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001506)

**[01:45:44.820] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001507)

**[01:45:47.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001508)  
If you look at the statistics of the BGNP and the BUCAR,

80% of the inmates inside the jails

**[01:45:54.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001510)

**[01:45:55.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001511)  
if on the market,

**[01:45:57.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001512)  
the male population.

**[01:45:59.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001513)  
80% are drug-related.

**[01:46:00.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001514)  
In the female population,

**[01:46:03.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001515)

**[01:46:05.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001516)

**[01:46:08.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001517)

**[01:46:10.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001518)

**[01:46:12.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001519)

**[01:46:13.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001520)

**[01:46:16.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001521)

**[01:46:20.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001522)

**[01:46:21.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001523)

**[01:46:26.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001524)

**[01:46:27.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001525)

**[01:46:31.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001526)

**[01:46:33.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001527)

**[01:46:35.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001528)

**[01:46:40.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001529)

**[01:46:41.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001530)

**[01:46:44.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001531)

**[01:46:47.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001532)

**[01:46:52.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001533)

**[01:46:54.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001534)

**[01:46:57.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001535)

**[01:47:01.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001536)

**[01:47:02.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001537)

**[01:47:05.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001538)  
Because what happens now

**[01:47:05.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001539)

**[01:47:06.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001540)

**[01:47:08.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001541)

**[01:47:10.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001542)

**[01:47:11.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001543)

**[01:47:12.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001544)

**[01:47:14.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001545)

**[01:47:15.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001546)

**[01:47:17.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001547)

**[01:47:18.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001548)

**[01:47:22.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001550)

**[01:47:25.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001551)  
So a reformation program

**[01:47:28.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001552)

**[01:47:29.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001553)

**[01:47:30.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001554)

**[02:00:33.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002103)

**[02:00:35.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002104)  
Ang tanong ko po, hindi ba po pwedeng bumili po ng vehicle ng sea transport, yung RD natin?

**[02:00:43.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002105)  
From Mindoro, pwede niya po mag-inspection sa Rumblon, Marinduque, Palawan,

**[02:00:51.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002106)  
para hindi na po siya pupunta ng Maynila?
