Ay.

**[02:00:56.210] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002108)  
Yes, Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[02:00:57.410] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002109)  
That is the, one of the consideration for the programming of our procurement of motor vehicle

**[02:01:06.210] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002110)  
as well as for our water crops, as well as the corresponding maintenances.

**[02:01:15.510] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002111)  
Sir, if I may.

**[02:01:17.050] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002112)  
Sorry.

**[02:01:17.850] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002113)  
Sir, we find that if it were part of our wish list, we would like to reform the concept of region,

**[02:01:26.210] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002114)  
Region 4B, and revise the entire system as it is.

**[02:01:31.490] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002115)  
As it is, Sir, Region 4B, its base is in Calapan, Negro Eye, and Mindoro.

**[02:01:38.290] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002116)  
But if you go there, none of the state offices are located in Calapan.

**[02:01:45.710] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002117)  
Like, even the ALG 4B, their main office is located in Quezon City.

**[02:01:56.210] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002118)  
The ALH is in Calamba.

**[02:01:58.390] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002119)  
The SWD is not there also.

**[02:02:00.670] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002120)  
Sir, what we propose is that we break up region, we bring it back to Region 4 into one region,

**[02:02:07.350] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002121)  
but divide it into different command centers.

**[02:02:11.290] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002122)  
What we would propose is that Cavite and Palawan be one region, one sub-region,

**[02:02:17.690] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002123)  
Batangas and Mindoro, one region, Laguna and Rizal, one region,

**[02:02:22.270] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002124)  
and then Quezon and Marinduque and Rumblon.

**[02:02:26.210] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002125)  
They will all be one region.

**[02:02:28.210] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002126)  
In that way, Sir, the government functions will be more rationalized.

**[02:02:36.770] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002127)  
As of now, Sir, it's almost impossible to be command or to be regional director of any agency to be in 4B.

**[02:02:45.970] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002128)  
And no one is in Calapan, except for the PNP.

**[02:02:50.770] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002129)  
But outside that, even agriculture, you were once part of the SWD, they're here in Manila.

**[02:02:55.470] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002130)  
Everyone is in Manila.

**[02:02:56.610] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002131)  
So I suggest that we break up that region to make it more responsive to the times.

**[02:03:01.070] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002132)  
So when do you plan to do that?

**[02:03:03.430] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002133)  
We will propose it to the Senate this coming, this year, Sir, and hopefully it will be supported.

**[02:03:10.330] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002134)  
Thank you, Po.

**[02:03:11.630] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002135)  
Another thing, Sec, yung ating pong PNP Maritime, kailangan po nila ng mga fast craft, lalo po sa Palawan,

**[02:03:18.950] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002136)  
because I came from Palawan, panghabol po sa mga illegal fishermen.

**[02:03:24.050] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002137)  
Medyo nahihirapan po sila.

**[02:03:25.470] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002138)  
Ilang po mga members, yung sasakyan po ng maritime, minsan hindi na po gumagana, problema po, pyesa.

**[02:03:31.830] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002139)  
Yung iba naman, gumagawa ng paraan, sumasakay po sa pump boat.

**[02:03:35.650] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002140)  
E hindi sila makahabol po sa mga illegal fishermen, mga basnig, mga twin engine pa.

**[02:03:41.350] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002141)  
So what do you plan to do this?

**[02:03:43.070] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002142)  
Are you going to procure more sea transport for our faster vehicle or sea vessel for our PNP Maritime?

**[02:03:53.550] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002143)  
No, Sir.

**[02:03:55.470] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002144)  
Our strategy is the rationalization of the Philippine Coast Guard at the maritime.

**[02:04:00.870] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002145)  
We believe that the Philippine Coast Guard, which is currently under the DOTR, should be under the DILG.

**[02:04:08.050] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002146)  
If the Coast Guard is under the DILG, because they are in charge of law enforcement in the seas,

**[02:04:13.290] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002147)  
then we can abrogate the entire maritime command of the PNP and have the Coast Guard be in charge of enforcement.

**[02:04:25.470] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002148)  
We would also like to suggest that the BGNP be transferred to the Bureau of Corrections

**[02:04:32.790] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002149)  
so that we will only have one correctional institution instead of two.

**[02:04:37.370] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002150)  
It's very confusing now, Sir.

**[02:04:40.190] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002151)  
Ibang BGNP, ibang BUCOR, dapat isa na lang yan, Sir.

**[02:04:44.510] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002152)  
So yung karpeta nila magkaiba.

**[02:04:46.970] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002153)  
So those are the things that we think we should do to rationalize the system of law enforcement in the country.

**[02:04:53.950] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002154)  
I have filed.

**[02:04:55.470] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002155)  
Already a bill on that matter, Sir, this year to transfer the BGNP to the Bureau of Corrections.

**[02:05:05.070] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002156)  
Yes, Sir.

**[02:05:05.630] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002157)  
It would also help, Sir, if the Philippine Coast Guard were transferred to the DILG

**[02:05:11.830] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002158)  
so that the law enforcement would all be under one department.

**[02:05:15.890] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002159)  
Sir, another aspect, Sir, is that we are the only country in the ASEAN that the Bureau of Immigration is under the Department of Justice.

**[02:05:25.270] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002160)  
Enough.

**[02:05:25.470] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002161)  
Not under the Department of the Interior.

**[02:05:27.970] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002162)  
As of now, Sir, when we go after foreign nationals, we have to go to the, the process is very cumbersome.

**[02:05:35.830] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002163)  
We have to go to the Bureau of Immigration, who has to go to the Department of Justice,

**[02:05:40.870] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002164)  
who will then give authorization to the Bureau of Immigration, who will then go back to the PNP.

**[02:05:46.390] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002165)  
So our response time for fugitives or for foreigners is more difficult.

**[02:05:51.710] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002166)  
So those are the rationalizations we propose, Sir.

**[02:05:54.850] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002167)  
With your permission.

**[02:05:55.470] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002168)  
Thank you, Senator Tulfo.

**[02:05:57.370] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002169)  
Have you recommended to the President?

**[02:05:59.510] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002170)  
Yes, Sir.

**[02:06:00.510] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002171)  
And he has expressed support.

**[02:06:02.290] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002172)  
We have lawyers here, and I believe it will only take an EO under the administrative code.

**[02:06:08.590] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002173)  
Yes, Sir.

**[02:06:09.050] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002174)  
Hindi kailangan ng legislation.

**[02:06:11.050] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002175)  
So if the President is, you know, is in agreement with the proposal,

**[02:06:16.450] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002176)  
maybe you can just drop an EO for the President and the BGNP may be transferred to...

**[02:06:23.070] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002177)  
Yes, Sir.

**[02:06:23.670] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002178)  
We have done so.

**[02:06:25.470] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002179)  
Thank you, Sir.

**[02:06:25.990] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002180)  
Thank you.

**[02:06:28.030] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002181)  
Hopefully, he said he will act on it already, so hopefully it will be done soon.

**[02:06:31.790] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002182)  
Thank you.

**[02:06:33.330] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002183)  
I'm Mr. Chairman with the indulgence of Senator Tulfo.

**[02:06:35.850] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002184)  
With regards to the maritime, sa PNP Maritime, we have equipped the PNP Maritime recently, no?

**[02:06:43.670] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002185)  
With new vessels, no?

**[02:06:46.670] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002186)  
Yung pong mga rib, yung mga rigid hull inflatable boats.

**[02:06:50.330] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002187)  
Kasi po dati, yung pong concern nyo na dati pag meron kinindap o hinahabol,

**[02:06:54.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002188)  
titignan lang sila Abu Sef, hindi sila makahabol kasi twin engine yung sa mga bandido.

**[02:07:01.550] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002189)  
Tapos yung sa atin, mabagal, hindi pa makagitna.

**[02:07:04.790] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002190)  
But recently, we were able to equip the maritime with new vessels.

**[02:07:10.190] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002191)  
Ang problema lang yung ano nito, yung the challenge is the DMOE, yung maintenance sila, no?

**[02:07:15.930] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002192)  
Kasi medyo mahal lang, ano eh.

**[02:07:17.710] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002193)  
Lalo yung fuel, lalo yung mga triple, ano to, di ba, tatlong engines nung minsan, yung ribs natin, yung mga rib boats.

**[02:07:23.830] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002194)  
So, yun lang.

**[02:07:24.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002195)  
And Mr. Chair, just to interject.

**[02:07:27.090] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002196)  
Thank you.

**[02:07:27.970] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002197)  
One last question po, Secretary John Vic.

**[02:07:31.330] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002198)  
Ilalapit ko na rin po sa inyo, tutal eh, mahilig po kayong baguhin at ayusin po yung sistema.

**[02:07:38.030] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002199)  
Kaya nga po, eh, natutuwa ako.

**[02:07:40.290] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002200)  
Ilalapit ko lang po sa inyo ito.

**[02:07:41.830] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002201)  
I don't know kung nakarating na po sa inyong kalaman.

**[02:07:44.730] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002202)  
Ito pong problema ng 911 emergency service na 2.45% lang po ang obligation rate.

**[02:07:52.550] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002203)  
Napakababa nga po.

**[02:07:54.810] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002204)  
Pero ang problema po kasi parang matagal pong sumagot.

**[02:07:58.290] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002205)  
Meron hong isang nagpadala po sa amin, si Basti Ascripa ito sa Facebook.

**[02:08:03.330] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002206)  
Pinoast niya.

**[02:08:04.730] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002207)  
Actually, nag-viral po ito, 1.7 million.

**[02:08:07.170] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002208)  
Namatay daw yung tatay niya kasi tunghawin sa 911, 10 minutes bago sumagot po.

**[02:08:12.130] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002209)  
At nung sumag pa, hinold po po siya.

**[02:08:14.890] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002210)  
Can you look into this, Sec?

**[02:08:16.770] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002211)  
I know you have the, may political will ho kayo at kaya niyong baguhin ito mga,

**[02:08:22.290] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002212)  
at risolbahin naman kayo itong klaseng problema.

**[02:08:24.810] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002213)  
Sir, I have the report here on hand.

**[02:08:27.730] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002214)  
I can submit it or I can read it.

**[02:08:30.390] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002215)  
Sir, the call was answered at approximately 12.15pm.

**[02:08:34.830] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002216)  
That was a waiting time of 1 minute and 25 seconds.

**[02:08:38.930] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002217)  
The biggest problem that we had there, sir, was that he gave a,

**[02:08:43.390] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002218)  
the address that he gave was a block number and street number and house number.

**[02:08:48.870] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002219)  
Where in Taguig, red has names of streets.

**[02:08:54.810] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002220)  
So, when we called the Taguig to, Taguig police or BFP to respond,

**[02:08:59.950] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002221)  
they couldn't find the address because there is no longer an address,

**[02:09:03.890] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002222)  
a record of where that block and street number was.

**[02:09:07.490] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002223)  
Eventually, they found it, but the barangay had reported ahead.

**[02:09:12.770] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002224)  
So, there was a communication gap and we have addressed that

**[02:09:17.330] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002225)  
and we are strengthening our response time.

**[02:09:19.770] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002226)  
But that was a fluke, sir.

**[02:09:22.190] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002227)  
That was one call that was...

**[02:09:24.810] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002228)  
that we have learned from and we are improving with.

**[02:09:28.990] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002229)  
Thank you, po.

**[02:09:30.110] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002230)  
Last two questions, sir, sir.

**[02:09:31.950] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002231)  
May plano po ba ang PNP na mag-augment pa po ng air assets para mas mabilis?

**[02:09:39.110] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002232)  
Given the situation, lalo na po sa Metro Manila na sobrang traffic,

**[02:09:43.590] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002233)  
mas mabilis po ang mobility kung meron pong air assets

**[02:09:46.990] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002234)  
ang Philippine National Police to respond, sir?

**[02:09:50.190] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002235)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:09:50.730] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002236)  
We already started the procurement of high, high...

**[02:09:54.810] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002237)  
quality drones instead of helicopters.

**[02:09:58.070] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002238)  
So, the operating cost of helicopters are at almost 80 to 100,000 pesos per hour of use.

**[02:10:07.330] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002239)  
The drones that we are purchasing, sir, can fly for 7 hours

**[02:10:10.430] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002240)  
at only a cost of 5,000 pesos per hour of use.

**[02:10:13.450] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002241)  
So, we are procuring, I think, 10 drones to patrol the entire country.

**[02:10:20.110] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002242)  
And I think it will be equipped with the latest infrared cameras,

**[02:10:24.810] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002243)  
and telescopes that can locate and help and augment in the peacekeeping.

**[02:10:29.950] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002244)  
So, if we call 911, we have an air asset on the sky,

**[02:10:35.210] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002245)  
and it can go immediately on top of the crime scene and give a report on what is going on.

**[02:10:39.330] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002246)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:10:40.070] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002247)  
Secretary, please continue po yung ginagawa ninyo mga changes sa inyo pong departamento and attach agency.

**[02:10:46.790] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002248)  
You are doing a good job. Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:10:48.470] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002249)  
Thank you very much, sir.

**[02:10:49.130] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002250)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:10:50.210] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002251)  
Thank you, Senator Tulfo.

**[02:10:52.650] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002252)  
Siguro, with regards to that, sir, we will support you.

**[02:10:54.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002253)  
Yung acquisition of drones, that's more practical for surveillance and monitoring.

**[02:11:01.710] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002254)  
But I think yung pong sa, Mr. Chair, yung pong sa, with the use of the helicopters,

**[02:11:06.790] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002255)  
I think, suggestion nyo natin is to disperse it to the different regions

**[02:11:10.730] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002256)  
so that yung presence will be there.

**[02:11:15.230] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002257)  
Kasi there, I still feel, think that there are still functions,

**[02:11:19.750] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002258)  
that the helicopter,

**[02:11:24.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002259)  
so like medical evacuation,

**[02:11:28.310] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002260)  
search and rescue,

**[02:11:30.470] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002261)  
tsaka pag-rescue mismo, lalo na calamity prone.

**[02:11:33.610] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002262)  
So we have allocated budget for construction of hangars in Cebu, Davao,

**[02:11:40.130] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002263)  
and then probably, and also La Union,

**[02:11:42.310] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002264)  
and probably you can add more so that ma-deploy na sila.

**[02:11:45.450] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002265)  
Kasi tama naman yung concern na pag nandito na sa Manila yung choppers,

**[02:11:50.410] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002266)  
it's just being used for VIP.

**[02:11:52.090] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002267)  
So I think it will be used for police.

**[02:11:54.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002268)  
Ano na talaga functions?

**[02:11:56.250] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002269)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:11:56.910] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002270)  
I also recommend a change of model, sir, from the Airbus to the MC-10, I think, or MC-11,

**[02:12:04.390] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002271)  
the model, because as of current,

**[02:12:07.590] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002272)  
the ones that we have are personal transport vehicles.

**[02:12:11.510] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002273)  
We cannot convert it for medical evacuation.

**[02:12:14.470] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002274)  
So we have to change the model of the helicopter, sir,

**[02:12:16.790] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002275)  
and we will distribute it around the country if we were given the budget to do so.

**[02:12:21.510] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002276)  
And I agree with Senator J.V. Sec.

**[02:12:24.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002277)  
Lalo na sa amin

**[02:12:26.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002278)  
doon sa Palawan, yung marami pong

**[02:12:28.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002279)  
nagkakaingin, yung mga ika nga namumutol po.

**[02:12:32.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002280)  
Nakikita mo from sa below,

**[02:12:34.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002281)  
sa baba, pero hindi naman po maakyat ng forest rangers natin.

**[02:12:38.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002282)  
So ang ginagawa po, pupuntahan, two days na lakaran.

**[02:12:42.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002283)  
Whereas kung meron nga po, lalapit po sila sa PNP, tatawag, mas mabilis po yung deployment.

**[02:12:47.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002284)  
Nakikita agad.

**[02:12:49.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002285)  
Unfortunately, hindi po mga ordinary farmers po yung nagkakaingin.

**[02:12:52.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002286)  
Yung mga malalaking

**[02:12:53.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002287)  
mga negosyo doon po

**[02:12:54.810] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002288)  
doon po nagpuputol ng kahoy, doon po nagsusunog kasi yan din po.

**[02:12:58.970] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002289)  
At saka itong pagpatrol po sa Sierra Madre, that's also another problem, yung mga illegal logging.

**[02:13:03.830] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002290)  
Yes sir, but the helicopters that we have now are incapable of that kind of infiltration method.

**[02:13:16.730] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002291)  
We have no mechanisms for drop-offs from the air to have ropes hanging from the helicopter.

**[02:13:23.910] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002292)  
So the ones we have now are general transport vehicles, but we need our specialized vehicles for police use.

**[02:13:31.770] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002293)  
And that's another model that I think Senator JB is very familiar with what we can use.

**[02:13:38.250] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002294)  
And just to give a report sir, I think we had purchased eight helicopters in the past.

**[02:13:45.190] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002295)  
And we have to restudy the maintenance procedures of the PNP and maybe outsource it.

**[02:13:53.050] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002296)  
Because in the...

**[02:13:53.890] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002297)  
In the last four years, three helicopters have crashed already.

**[02:13:59.270] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002298)  
And so we have to have a restudy on the core competency of the PNP in maintaining and operating helicopters.

**[02:14:11.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002299)  
With indulgence of the Senate President, puntala ko sa unfilled positions.

**[02:14:16.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002300)  
Yung total unfilled positions sa DILG, the entire DILG family is 17,000 plus, 17,622.

**[02:14:25.100] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002301)  
Before we...

**[02:14:26.640] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002302)  
Go to the breakdown of numbers.

**[02:14:31.920] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002303)  
Ang tanong ko lang po, meron ba kayong recruitment plan, pati yung inyong year-end quota?

**[02:14:39.690] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002304)  
Say for 2027, kasi most of the unfilled positions, nasa mga uniformed services.

**[02:14:45.750] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002305)  
PNP, BJMP, BFP.

**[02:14:49.710] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002306)  
So anong breakdown nito and ano yung inyong year-end quota as per your recruitment plan?

**[02:14:57.530] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002307)  
Sir?

**[02:14:58.150] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002308)  
Sir, ang OSEC, sir.

**[02:15:00.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002309)  
Yes, 555 unfilled positions.

**[02:15:04.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002310)  
Fill-up rate po namin is 88.87%.

**[02:15:08.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002311)  
Naglagay po kami ng mga JO kasi parami hindi qualified sa applicants

**[02:15:14.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002312)  
at hindi tiyatanggap yung sweldo na commensurate sa pinag-aralan nila.

**[02:15:21.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002313)  
So, we have to have a government optimization program for this

**[02:15:25.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002314)  
and rethink the structure that we have.

**[02:15:30.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002315)  
The PNP, sir, has 10,041 unfilled positions.

**[02:15:37.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002316)  
And you're requesting for 2,000 to be filled up, diba?

**[02:15:41.800] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002317)  
For both PNP and BFP, 2,000 for 2027.

**[02:15:48.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002318)  
Yes, sir. Those are for the new recruits, sir. Yes.

**[02:15:52.040] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002319)  
PNP ganun din, 2,000 for 2027.

**[02:15:55.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002320)  
Ang mahirap kasi, sir, is recruitment alone is very difficult.

**[02:16:00.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002321)  
It's a process to get qualified people to be policemen.

**[02:16:03.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002322)  
Andami yung, I think we have a 70% failure rate sa applicants.

**[02:16:08.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002323)  
Andami nag-a-apply pero hindi talaga pumapas eh.

**[02:16:11.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002324)  
So, doon ho kami nahihirapan talaga sa how to fill it up.

**[02:16:15.440] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002325)  
Yeah, related to that, ano yung per capita recruitment cost?

**[02:16:21.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002326)  
Per capita recruitment cost?

**[02:16:25.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002327)  
I'll give you a minute, sir.

**[02:16:32.390] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002328)  
16,000 per police, sir.

**[02:16:35.510] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002329)  
And yung salary grade ng PNP?

**[02:16:38.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002330)  
Entry, no? Bagong entry.

**[02:16:42.210] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002331)  
Nasa mga 31,000, diba?

**[02:16:44.490] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002332)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:16:44.890] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002333)  
Both BFP and PNP.

**[02:16:47.410] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002334)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:16:48.110] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002335)  
Kasi yung numbers don't add up, ano.

**[02:16:50.330] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002336)  
Yung sa BFP submission, nangingin silang 2,000 additional fire officer 1.

**[02:16:58.010] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002337)  
Ang hiningi nila is 17,717 million pesos.

**[02:17:02.390] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002338)  
Ang sa inyong presentation, ang binigay ninyo is 697.

**[02:17:08.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002339)  
Pag dinivide po natin yun, sa 2,000, lumalabas na sa mga 29,000 plus lang eh, per month.

**[02:17:20.790] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002340)  
So, sino mag-aabono? Kasi 31,000 yung sweldo, yung entry level.

**[02:17:28.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002341)  
Or, meron kayong allowance for yung failure.

**[02:17:32.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002342)  
Pero hindi, recruitment na ito eh.

**[02:17:34.320] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002343)  
Ang assumption dito is recruited.

**[02:17:35.860] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002344)  
So, if you're recruiting, ang program nyo is to recruit 2,000.

**[02:17:40.240] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002345)  
2,000 police officers and 2,000 BFP.

**[02:17:45.900] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002346)  
Bakit may discrepancy sa total? Parang kulang yata.

**[02:18:00.490] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002347)  
Anyway, paki-reconcile na lang yung mga numbers?

**[02:18:02.810] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002348)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:18:04.690] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002349)  
We will, in the next budget revision, we will give the complete answer, sir.

**[02:18:09.470] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002350)  
Maybe sa plenary na lang?

**[02:18:10.190] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002351)  
Sa plenary, sir.

**[02:18:11.110] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002352)  
Thank you.

**[02:18:14.470] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002353)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:18:16.410] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002354)  
Mr. Chairman, just a few more questions.

**[02:18:21.190] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002355)  
Kumusta yung ating pulis?

**[02:18:23.070] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002356)  
Pulis to population ratio, to Secretary and to the Chief PNP.

**[02:18:28.050] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002357)  
How are we doing with the ideal?

**[02:18:33.250] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002358)  
Ang ideal if under Republic Act 6795 is 500 per one.

**[02:18:42.590] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002359)  
So, kumusta yung ating ratio?

**[02:18:46.190] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002360)  
And sabi nga ng ating chairman ni Senator Ping, iba rin yung nagiging ratio sa urban.

**[02:18:53.070] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002361)  
Iba rin yung nagiging ratio sa rural, no?

**[02:18:55.530] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002362)  
But I think system-wide, ano po ang ating ratio ngayon?

**[02:19:01.330] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002363)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair, the ideal ratio is 1 is to 5,000.

**[02:19:07.670] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002364)  
1 is to 500.

**[02:19:10.290] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002365)  
Right now, it is pegged at 1 is to 692.

**[02:19:15.190] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002366)  
So, if we will have to compute it, we need...

**[02:19:24.610] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002367)  
We need a total of...

**[02:19:28.550] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002368)  
How many do we need to reach the 1 is to 500 ratio?

**[02:19:43.230] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002369)  
Yes.

**[02:19:46.990] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002370)  
Right now, we are 130,000 and the ideal is we should be at 271,000, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[02:19:57.770] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002371)  
271,000.

**[02:19:58.930] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002393)  
Sir, we need 49,000 additional policemen.

**[02:20:03.350] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002394)  
Mr. President, realistically, ito yung the entire police population, di ba?

**[02:20:12.990] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002395)  
Pero if we consider yung deployed, yung front line against those staying in the headquarters,

**[02:20:20.510] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002396)  
hindi 1 is to 650 yan.

**[02:20:22.150] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002397)  
Ay, mas mali ito, sir.

**[02:20:24.190] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002398)  
Mas lalaki pa yung...

**[02:20:25.230] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002399)  
Lalaki pa pala yung ratio.

**[02:20:27.050] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002400)  
Assuming na 15% ang nasa headquarters,

**[02:20:30.850] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002401)  
85% yung nasa labas.

**[02:20:33.290] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002402)  
So ano na yung ratio?

**[02:20:36.310] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002403)  
We would have to study that, sir, kasi ang nangyayari, sir, in any municipality,

**[02:20:42.550] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002404)  
a number of them become security personnel of governors, mayors,

**[02:20:49.510] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002405)  
even counselors and baragay captains request police.

**[02:20:54.550] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002406)  
And senators?

**[02:20:55.910] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002407)  
There are only 24 senators, sir, so it's not that big a problem.

**[02:21:00.310] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002408)  
The bigger problem is that there are 82 provinces and there are 169 cities and so many municipalities

**[02:21:09.830] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002409)  
and they are dispersed as bodyguards of a lot of these politicians, sir.

**[02:21:15.190] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002410)  
Again, with the permission of the Senate President, alam ni Chief Tating ito, no?

**[02:21:21.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002411)  
Di ba dati tayo 1585?

**[02:21:24.270] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002412)  
Mga pa logistics, mga pa finance, mga pa personnel.

**[02:21:28.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002413)  
Hanggang ngayon ba 1585?

**[02:21:30.310] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002414)  
At all levels, lahat ng headquarters level, nasa 15% lang yung nasa headquarters.

**[02:21:38.390] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002415)  
Against deployed, yung actually deployed sa field?

**[02:21:43.770] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002416)  
Yes, Your Honor. That's the policy as of now for the deployment.

**[02:21:47.830] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002417)  
And we even stretch it as to 90-10, 90%-10% deployment na nasa headquarters and actually on the street.

**[02:22:00.310] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002418)  
Yes, Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[02:22:02.390] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002419)  
And with regards to the actual number that we needed,

**[02:22:05.890] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002420)  
with the number of personnel yearly,

**[02:22:10.970] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002421)  
approximately, we are filling up the Philippine National Police by recruiting about 2,000 PNP personnel.

**[02:22:22.130] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002422)  
And hindi lang naman yun ang kailangan natin if we will stop from recruiting personnel.

**[02:22:28.230] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002423)  
Kaya nga meron tayong...

**[02:22:30.310] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002424)  
To equip them, such that this will be the force multipliers.

**[02:22:34.850] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002425)  
Like if we have 100 personnel in a police station, mas maganda rin na meron tayong equipage.

**[02:22:45.350] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002426)  
Like the needed radio, the needed mobility, the needed CCTVs on the street, and others.

**[02:22:53.830] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002427)  
Such that this 100 personnel on a police station or a...

**[02:23:00.310] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002428)  
Kaya munisipality, magdo-double or triple ito, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[02:23:06.430] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002429)  
Kung i-juxtapose natin ito sa atrisyon, yung 2,000 mo against yung nagre-retire na mamatay on a yearly basis,

**[02:23:16.390] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002430)  
ano maging effective number?

**[02:23:20.450] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002431)  
About 50% of that, Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[02:23:24.150] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002432)  
Mag-recruit ka ng 2,000, pero actually 1,000 lang yung warm bodies mo doon.

**[02:23:30.310] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002433)  
That's correct, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[02:23:33.450] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002434)  
Thank you. Thank you, Mr. President.

**[02:23:35.470] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002435)  
Mr. Chair, to answer your question regarding the BFP discrepancies that you saw.

**[02:23:40.290] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002436)  
The 2,000 additional fire officer, one salary is 717,179,000.

**[02:23:46.650] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002437)  
But there will be a discrepancy because they will enter the service sometime March, April, or May.

**[02:23:53.210] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002438)  
So, in the end, mas maliit ang magiging siyoldo ng lalabas na siyoldo.

**[02:23:57.130] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002439)  
Hindi 12 months.

**[02:23:58.150] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002440)  
Hindi pa 12 months.

**[02:23:59.190] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002441)  
Thank you. Now I understand.

**[02:24:00.310] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002442)  
Okay, sir.

**[02:24:02.050] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002443)  
Sir Chair, singit na ako, no?

**[02:24:04.490] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002444)  
Yung sinabi ni Secretary Mulya, I attest to that, no?

**[02:24:08.230] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002445)  
Marami talaga nagsiserve as security to local government officials.

**[02:24:14.070] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002446)  
Kaya yung sinabi ni Senator Lacson na 85-15.

**[02:24:17.970] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002447)  
Maganda sana kung ganun na 85 nasa field and then 15 lang nasa office.

**[02:24:22.650] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002448)  
Kaya ano nangyayari?

**[02:24:24.370] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002449)  
Sa aming nga sa San Juan, parang 85 nasa field, yung 15 nasa mayor.

**[02:24:30.310] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002450)  
Diba?

**[02:24:31.130] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002451)  
Pati anak, lahat ng anak, asawa, lahat.

**[02:24:34.430] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002452)  
At sa siya, ang dami niya.

**[02:24:36.130] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002453)  
So, dapat bumalik tayo siguro, if you could be strict about it, I think, PSPG, dapat one or two lang allowed, no?

**[02:24:45.050] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002454)  
Yung point.

**[02:24:46.050] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002455)  
And it happens all over the country, you know?

**[02:24:49.030] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002456)  
Ito may escort-escort pa, di ba? Lapit-lapit.

**[02:24:51.690] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002457)  
So, sayang.

**[02:24:53.110] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002458)  
Because I think that as local officials, hayaan natin that the police, we need more foot soldiers.

**[02:25:00.310] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002459)  
On the ground, di ba?

**[02:25:02.170] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002460)  
Na hindi nandun lang nagbabantay sa kanila.

**[02:25:05.410] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002461)  
So, totoo po yan.

**[02:25:06.330] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002462)  
I attest to that.

**[02:25:08.070] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002463)  
That it really happens, especially the provinces.

**[02:25:14.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002464)  
Buti, wala na nga tayong Pogo.

**[02:25:15.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002465)  
Dati may Pogo pa.

**[02:25:17.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002466)  
May pa-isang naging problema.

**[02:25:20.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002467)  
Nagiging bodyguard ng Pogo.

**[02:25:22.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002468)  
Narinig ko, Secretary, that we need to recruit, to bring it down to 500.

**[02:25:27.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002469)  
One is to 500.

**[02:25:29.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002470)  
We need to recruit 49,000.

**[02:25:31.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002471)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:25:32.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002472)  
But tinitignan ko yung unfilled position ng PNP.

**[02:25:36.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002473)  
Ang base dito sa amin, ang unfilled position is only 9,000.

**[02:25:42.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002474)  
So, in other words, funded, 9,000.

**[02:25:45.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002475)  
But we actually need 49,000.

**[02:25:48.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002476)  
So, in other words, kulang implantilia items.

**[02:25:52.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002477)  
Tama po ba?

**[02:25:54.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002478)  
Tama po ba yung aking computation?

**[02:25:57.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002479)  
So, in other words, hindi natin marireach yung 500.

**[02:26:01.860] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002480)  
One is to 500.

**[02:26:02.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002481)  
Kasi kulang tayo sa plantili items.

**[02:26:06.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002482)  
Tama po ba?

**[02:26:07.300] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002483)  
That's correct, Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[02:26:10.500] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002484)  
That's why we are also looking after or drafting our OSSP one after, in every unit of the Philippine National Police.

**[02:26:20.520] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002485)  
For example, we have, when I assume 13 months ago,

**[02:26:26.020] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002486)  
I make it sure na yung Philippine National Police ay ayusin natin.

**[02:26:31.360] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002487)  
At,

**[02:26:32.520] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002488)  
dun po sa Tagaytay or Silangkabite.

**[02:26:34.720] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002489)  
So, right now, it's already at the National Police Commission,

**[02:26:39.460] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002490)  
such that yung organization and structure of the PNPA

**[02:26:46.380] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002491)  
ay finally, dun pa lang, simula, dun pa lang sa organization,

**[02:26:51.180] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002492)  
ay maayos na po natin to fill up the needed professors,

**[02:26:55.600] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002493)  
the tactical officers, and other, sir, including yung mga utilities doon.

**[02:27:02.520] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002494)  
That soon will be part of the increase of budget for the PNP.

**[02:27:12.800] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002495)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[02:27:14.180] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002496)  
I will further complicate your problem.

**[02:27:17.980] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002497)  
Pag sa police to population ratio,

**[02:27:21.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002498)  
kinukonsider nyo ba yung daytime population against the actual population?

**[02:27:25.780] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002499)  
Makati, for example, ang population nila ay 309,000.

**[02:27:30.080] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002500)  
Okay.

**[02:27:30.900] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002501)  
Pero ang daytime population is time.

**[02:27:32.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002502)  
10 times, 3 million.

**[02:27:35.500] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002503)  
So is that

**[02:27:36.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002504)  
factored in when you deploy

**[02:27:38.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002505)  
policemen or

**[02:27:40.740] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002506)  
when you assign policemen sa field?

**[02:27:43.480] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002507)  
Kasi pagka 3.2

**[02:27:44.920] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002508)  
million yung in-address mo,

**[02:27:47.460] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002509)  
kung 1 is to 500,

**[02:27:49.740] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002510)  
medyo

**[02:27:50.160] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002511)  
lopsided na siguro yung

**[02:27:52.200] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002512)  
ratio niya. Ano na?

**[02:27:54.960] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-002513)  
Yes, Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[02:27:57.400] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002514)  
Yan po yung consideration.

**[02:27:59.280] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002515)  
For example, if I were going to

**[02:28:00.700] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-002516)  
compare PRO4A

**[02:29:13.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002552)

**[02:29:26.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002559)  
So baligtad, baligtad ang problema nun.

**[02:29:29.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002560)  
Bedroom community.

**[02:29:32.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002561)  
Bedroom province.

**[02:29:33.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002562)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:29:35.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002563)  
Kay Chief PNP, pare-view na lang

**[02:29:37.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002564)
